/ neighbour decomposition of Nazarov, Treil and Volberg
[
NTV4
]
using
Haar wavelets.

When using weighted Alpert wavelet projections
\bigtriangleup_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma}
instead, the projection
\mathbb{E}_{I^{\prime};\kappa}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma}f
onto the child
I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)
equals
M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}\mathbf{1}_{I_{\pm}}
where
M=M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}
is a
polynomial of degree less than
\kappa
restricted to
I^{\prime}
, and
hence no longer commutes with the operator
T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}
- unless
it is the constant polynomial. We now recall the modifications used in
[
AlSaUr
]
, where they obtained,

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\equiv\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left\langle T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\bigtriangleup_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma}f,\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\mathbf{\tau}-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left\langle T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\left(\mathbf{1}_{I_{J}}\bigtriangleup_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma}f\right),\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\mathbf{\tau}-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\sum_{\theta\left(I_{J}\right)\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)\setminus\left\{I_{J}\right\}}\left\langle T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\left(\mathbf{1}_{\theta\left(I_{J}\right)}\bigtriangleup_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma}f\right),\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{h}ome};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)+\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{n}eighbour};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right).

They further decomposed the
\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{h}ome};\kappa}^{F}
form
using

(6.1)

M_{I^{\prime}}=M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}\equiv\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\bigtriangleup_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma}f=\mathbb{E}_{I^{\prime};\kappa}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma}f,

to obtain

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{h}ome};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left\langle T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\left(M_{I_{J}}\mathbf{1}_{I_{J}}\right),\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left\langle M_{I_{J}}T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{I_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left\langle\left[T_{\sigma}^{\lambda},M_{I_{J}}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left\langle M_{I_{J}}T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{F},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}-\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left\langle M_{I_{J}}T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus I_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left\langle\left[T_{\sigma}^{\lambda},M_{I_{J}}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I_{J}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{p}araproduct};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)-\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)+\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{c}ommutator};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right).

Altogether then we have the weighted Alpert version of the Nazarov, Treil
and Volberg paraproduct decomposition that was obtained by Alexis, Sawyer
and Uriarte-Tuero in
[
AlSaUr
]
,

\mathsf{B}_{\Subset_{\mathbf{\rho},\varepsilon};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)=\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{p}araproduct};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)+\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)+\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{c}ommutator};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)+\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{n}eighbour};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right).

6.1.
The paraproduct form

Following
[
AlSaUr
]
, we first pigeonhole the sum over pairs
I
and
J

arising in the paraproduct form according to which child
K\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)
contains
J
,

\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{p}araproduct};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)=\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}}\sum_{K\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}:\ J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\\
J\subset K\end{subarray}}\left\langle M_{K;\kappa}T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{F},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}.

The form
\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{p}araproduct};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)

will be handled using the telescoping property in part (2) of Theorem
10
, to sum the restrictions to a cube
J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}
of the polynomials
M_{K;\kappa}
on a child
K\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)
of
I
, over the relevant cubes
I
, to obtain a restricted polynomial
\mathbf{1}_{J}P_{K;\kappa}
that is
controlled by
E_{F}^{\sigma}\left|f\right|
, and then passing
the polynomial
M_{J;\kappa}
over to
\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g
. More precisely, for each
J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\mathbf{\tau}-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}
, let
I_{J}^{\natural}
denote the smallest
L\in\mathcal{C}_{F}

such that
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}L
provided it exists. Note that
J

is at most
\tau
levels below the bottom of the corona
\mathcal{C}_{F}
,
and since
\rho>2\tau
, we have that
either

\pi_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(\rho\right)}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}

or
that
\pi_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(\rho\right)}J\supsetneqq F
. Let
I_{J}^{\flat}
denote the
\mathcal{D}
-child of
I_{J}^{\natural}
that contains
J
, provided
I_{J}^{\natural}
exists. We have

(6.2)

\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}:\ I_{J}^{\natural}\subset I}\mathbf{1}_{J}M_{K;\kappa}=\mathbf{1}_{J}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}:\ I_{J}^{\natural}\subset I}M_{K;\kappa}=\mathbf{1}_{J}\left(\mathbb{E}_{I_{J}^{\flat};\kappa}^{\sigma}f-\mathbb{E}_{F;\kappa}^{\sigma}f\right)\equiv\left\{\begin{array}[]{ccc}\mathbf{1}_{J}P_{J;\kappa}&\text{ if }&I_{J}^{\natural}\text{ exists}\\
0&\text{ if }&I_{J}^{\natural}\text{ doesn't exist}\end{array}\right..

Then we write

\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{p}araproduct};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{, }I_{J}^{\flat}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\mathbf{\rho},\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left\langle M_{I_{J};\kappa}T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{F},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\ .

From (
6.2
) we obtain

(6.3)

\left\|\mathbf{1}_{J}P_{J;\kappa}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\sigma\right)}\leq\left\|\mathbb{E}_{I_{J}^{\flat};\kappa}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\sigma\right)}+\left\|\mathbb{E}_{F;\kappa}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\sigma\right)},

and so

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{p}araproduct};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\left\langle\mathbf{1}_{J}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}:\ I_{J}^{\flat}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\\
J\Subset_{\mathbf{\rho},\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)\\
J\subset I^{\prime}\text{ }\end{subarray}}M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}\right)T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{F},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\left\langle\mathbf{1}_{J}P_{J;\kappa}T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{F},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{p}araproduct};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\left\langle T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{F},P_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|=\left|\left\langle T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{F},\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}P_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{F}\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{s}\left(\mu\right)}\left\|\mathbb{E}_{F;\kappa}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\frac{P_{J;\kappa}}{E_{F}^{\sigma}\left|f\right|}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\mathfrak{T}_{T^{\lambda}}^{s}\ell\left(F\right)^{-s}\sqrt{\left|F\right|_{\sigma}}\left\|\mathbb{E}_{F;\kappa}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\frac{P_{J;\kappa}}{E_{F}^{\sigma}\left|f\right|}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}.

Now we will use an almost orthogonality argument that reflects the fact that
for
J^{\prime}
small compared to
J
, the function
M_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g
has vanishing
\omega
-mean, and the polynomial
\mathbf{1}_{J}P_{J;\kappa}^{\mathop{\mathrm{c}orona}}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g
is relatively smooth at the scale
of
J^{\prime}
, together with the fact that the polynomials

R_{J;\kappa}\equiv\frac{P_{J;\kappa}}{E_{F}^{\sigma}\left|f\right|}

of degree at most
\kappa-1
, have
L^{\infty}
norm uniformly bounded by
the constant
C
appearing in (
6.3
). We begin by writing

\displaystyle\left\|\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}=\left\langle\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g,\sum_{J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J,J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J^{\prime}\subset J\end{subarray}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J,J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\subsetneqq J^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\right\}\left\langle R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g,R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}\equiv A+B.

By symmetry, it suffices to estimate term
A
. We will use the definitions

\displaystyle\left\|f\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{s}\left(\mu\right)}^{2}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{D}}\ell\left(Q\right)^{-2s}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{Q;\kappa}^{\mu}f\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\mu\right)}^{2},

\displaystyle\left\langle f,h\right\rangle_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{s}\left(\mu\right)}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{Q\in\mathcal{D}}\ell\left(Q\right)^{-2s}\left\langle\bigtriangleup_{Q;\kappa}^{\mu}f,\bigtriangleup_{Q;\kappa}^{\mu}h\right\rangle_{L^{2}\left(\mu\right)},

together with the fact that when
\kappa=1
,
\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g
has one vanishing
moment, to obtain

\displaystyle A

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J,J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J^{\prime}\subset J\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q\in\mathcal{D}\\
Q\subset J\end{subarray}}\ell\left(Q\right)^{2s}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left(\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)\left(\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)d\omega

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}=\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}=\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}.

Note that here it is important to know that
W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)
equals both
W_{\mathcal{D};\kappa}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)
and
W_{\mathcal{D};1}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)
.

Indeed, if
J^{\prime}
is small compared to
J
, and
J^{\prime}\subset J_{J^{\prime}}\subset J
, then there are just three possibilities for
Q
,
namely
Q\cap J^{\prime}=\emptyset
,
Q\supsetneqq J^{\prime}
, and
Q\subset J^{\prime}
. If
Q\cap J^{\prime}=\emptyset
then the integral
vanishes by support considerations. If
Q\supsetneqq J^{\prime}
then the
integral vanishes since
\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}\left(R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)
is constant on
J^{\prime}
,
R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g

has
\omega
-mean
0
on
J^{\prime}
, and
\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}
is a projection. Thus we are left with the case where
Q\subset J^{\prime}\subset J
. We have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left(\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}\left(R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)\right)\left(\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}\left(R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)\right)d\omega\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\|R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\times\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left(\frac{\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}\left(R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)}{\left\|R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}}\right)\left(\frac{\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}\left(R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)}{\left\|R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}}\right)d\omega\right|,

and now if
Q\subset J^{\prime}
, we get

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left(\frac{\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}\left(R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)}{\left\|R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}}\right)\left(\frac{\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}\left(R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)}{\left\|R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}}\right)d\omega

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left(\frac{\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g}{\left\|R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}}\right)\left(\frac{R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g}{\left\|R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}}\right)d\omega

where
\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}
has one vanishing mean, and hence

\displaystyle\left|\int\left(\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}\left(R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)\right)\left(\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}\left(R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)\right)d\omega\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{\ell\left(Q\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\left\|R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}\frac{\ell\left(Q\right)}{\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)}\left\|R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}\left|Q\right|_{\omega}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}}{\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\frac{\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}}{\sqrt{\left|J^{\prime}\right|_{\omega}}}\frac{\ell\left(Q\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\frac{\ell\left(Q\right)}{\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)}\left|Q\right|_{\omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\ell\left(Q\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\frac{\ell\left(Q\right)}{\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)}\sqrt{\frac{\left|Q\right|_{\omega}}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\sqrt{\frac{\left|Q\right|_{\omega}}{\left|J^{\prime}\right|_{\omega}}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{\left|Q\right|_{\omega}}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\frac{\ell\left(Q\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)},

by (
2.9
), i.e.

\displaystyle\left\|R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}\sqrt{\left|J^{\prime}\right|_{\omega}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}\sqrt{\left|J^{\prime}\right|_{\omega}}\lesssim\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)},

\displaystyle\left\|R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\omega\right)}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\lesssim\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}.

Note that it was necessary to invoke the Haar wavelets with
\kappa=1
in
order to obtain this inequality for
s\neq 0
.

We now note for use in the next estimate that

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q\in\mathcal{D}\\
Q\subset J^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\ell\left(Q\right)^{2s}\sqrt{\frac{\left|Q\right|_{\omega}}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\frac{\ell\left(Q\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}=\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q\in\mathcal{D}\\
Q\subset J^{\prime}\text{ and }\ell\left(Q\right)=2^{-m}\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)\end{subarray}}\ell\left(Q\right)^{2s}\sqrt{\frac{\left|Q\right|_{\omega}}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\frac{\ell\left(Q\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)^{2s}2^{-2sm}\frac{2^{-m}\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\sqrt{\frac{1}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q\in\mathcal{D}\\
Q\subset J^{\prime}\text{ and }\ell\left(Q\right)=2^{-m}\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)\end{subarray}}1\sqrt{\left|Q\right|_{\omega}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)^{2s}2^{-2sm}\frac{2^{-m}\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\sqrt{\frac{1}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}2^{\frac{m}{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q\in\mathcal{D}\\
Q\subset J^{\prime}\text{ and }\ell\left(Q\right)=2^{-m}\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)\end{subarray}}\left|Q\right|_{\omega}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)^{2s}\frac{\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\sqrt{\frac{\left|J^{\prime}\right|_{\omega}}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}2^{\frac{m}{2}}2^{-2sm}2^{-m}\leq C_{s}\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)^{2s}\frac{\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\sqrt{\frac{\left|J^{\prime}\right|_{\omega}}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}.

Thus recalling that we restricted attention to the case
J^{\prime}\subset J
by symmetry, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J,J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J^{\prime}\subset J\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q\in\mathcal{D}\\
Q\subset J\end{subarray}}\ell\left(Q\right)^{2s}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left(\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)\left(\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)d\omega\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J,J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J^{\prime}\subset J\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q\in\mathcal{D}\\
Q\subset J^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\ell\left(Q\right)^{2s}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left(\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)\left(\bigtriangleup_{Q;1}^{\omega}R_{J^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right)d\omega\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J,J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J^{\prime}\subset J\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}Q\in\mathcal{D}\\
Q\subset J^{\prime}\end{subarray}}\ell\left(Q\right)^{2s}\sqrt{\frac{\left|Q\right|_{\omega}}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\frac{\ell\left(Q\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J,J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J^{\prime}\subset J\end{subarray}}\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)^{2s}\frac{\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)}\sqrt{\frac{\left|J^{\prime}\right|_{\omega}}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}

which is

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}2^{-m}2^{-2sm}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J,J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)=2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\end{subarray}}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\sqrt{\frac{\left|J^{\prime}\right|_{\omega}}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}2^{-m}2^{-2sm}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\sum_{J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\mathbf{\tau}-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}:\ \ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)=2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)}\sqrt{\frac{\left|J^{\prime}\right|_{\omega}}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}2^{-m}2^{-2sm}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\sqrt{\sum_{J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\mathbf{\tau}-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}:\ \ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)=2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)}\frac{\left|J^{\prime}\right|_{\omega}}{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{J^{\prime}:\ \ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)=2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}}

which is at most

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}2^{-m}2^{-2sm}\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}\sqrt{\sum_{J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}:\ \ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)=2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}2^{-m}2^{-2sm}\sqrt{\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\sum_{J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}:\ \ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)=2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}}\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}2^{-m}2^{-2sm}2^{ms}\sqrt{\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\sum_{J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}:\ \ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)=2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)}\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)^{2s}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}2^{-m}2^{-2sm}2^{ms}\sqrt{\sum_{J^{\prime}\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\ell\left(J^{\prime}\right)^{2s}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J^{\prime};\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}}=C_{s}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}

provided that
s>-1
. Altogether we have shown

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{p}araproduct};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathfrak{T}_{T^{\lambda}}^{s}\ell\left(F\right)^{-s}\sqrt{\left|F\right|_{\sigma}}\left\|\mathbb{E}_{F;\kappa}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\frac{P_{J;\kappa}}{E_{F}^{\sigma}\left|f\right|}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathfrak{T}_{T^{\lambda}}^{s}\ell\left(F\right)^{-s}\sqrt{\left|F\right|_{\sigma}}\left\|\mathbb{E}_{F;\kappa}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\sigma\right)}C_{s}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}.

For future reference we record the fact that the main inequality proved
above,

\left\|\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}R_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}\lesssim\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2},

continues to hold if
\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}
is replaced
by an arbitrary subset
\mathcal{J}
of the dyadic grid
\mathcal{D}
, and
if the polynomials
R_{J;\kappa}
are replaced with any family of
polynomials
\left\{W_{J;\kappa}\right\}_{J\in\mathcal{J}}
such that
for all
J\in\mathcal{J}
,

\displaystyle\deg W_{J;\kappa}

\displaystyle<

\displaystyle\kappa,

\displaystyle\int_{J}W_{J;\kappa}d\omega

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 0,

\displaystyle\sup_{J}\left|W_{J;\kappa}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C.

More precisely the above arguments prove,

(6.4)

\left\|\sum_{J\in\mathcal{J}}W_{J;\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}\lesssim\sum_{J\in\mathcal{J}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}.

Since the weighted Sobolev wavelets
\left\{\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}\right\}_{J\in\mathcal{D}}
are pairwise orthogonal, we have

\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}=\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2},

and so we obtain

\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{p}araproduct};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\right|\lesssim\mathfrak{T}_{T^{\lambda}}^{s}\ell\left(F\right)^{-s}\left\|\mathbb{E}_{F;\kappa}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\sigma\right)}\sqrt{\left|F\right|_{\sigma}}\ \left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

as required by (
5.16
).

Finally using orthogonality of
\left\{\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}\right\}_{F\in\mathcal{F}}
and
quasiorthogonality in Lemma
30
, we obtain

(6.5)

\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{p}araproduct};\kappa}\left(f,g\right)\right|\lesssim\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\left\|f\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{s}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)},\ \ \ \ \ \left|s\right|<1.

We next turn to the commutator inner products
\left\langle\left[T_{\sigma}^{\lambda},M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}
arising
in
\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{c}ommutator};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)
,
followed by the neighbour and stopping inner products.

Remark 49
.

The arguments for the commutator, neighbour and stopping forms follow
closely the analogous arguments in
[
AlSaUr
]
where the case
s=0
is
handled. Nevertheless, there are differences arising when
s\neq 0
, and so
we give complete details for the convenience of the reader.

6.2.
The commutator form

Fix
\kappa\geq 1
. In this subsection we use
\alpha
to denote a
multiindex in
\mathbb{R}^{n}
, and so we will instead use
\lambda
to
denote the fractional order of the Calderon-Zygmund operator. Assume now
that
K^{\lambda}
is a general standard
\lambda
-fractional kernel in
\mathbb{R}^{n}
, and
T^{\lambda}
is the associated Calderón-Zygmund
operator, and that
P_{\alpha,a,I^{\prime}}\left(x\right)=\left(\frac{x-a}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\alpha}=\left(\frac{x_{1}-a_{1}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\alpha_{1}}...\left(\frac{x_{n}-a_{n}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\alpha_{n}}
,
where
\left|\alpha\right|\leq\kappa-1
and
I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)
,
I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}
. We
recall from
[
AlSaUr
]
the formula

x^{\alpha}-y^{\alpha}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(x_{k}-y_{k}\right)\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{\left|\beta\right|+\left|\gamma\right|=\left|\alpha\right|-1}c_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}x^{\beta}y^{\gamma}.

Continuing to follow
[
AlSaUr
]
, we then have

(6.6)

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\left(x\right)\left[P_{\alpha,a,I^{\prime}}\ ,T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\left(x\right)=\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\left(x\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}K^{\lambda}\left(x-y\right)\left\{P_{\alpha,a,I^{\prime}}\left(x\right)-P_{\alpha,a,I^{\prime}}\left(y\right)\right\}\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\left(y\right)d\sigma\left(y\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\left(x\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}K^{\lambda}\left(x-y\right)\left\{\sum_{k=1}^{n}\left(\frac{x_{k}-y_{k}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{\left|\beta\right|+\left|\gamma\right|=\left|\alpha\right|-1}c_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}\left(\frac{x-a}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\beta}\left(\frac{y-a}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\gamma}\right\}\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\left(y\right)d\sigma\left(y\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{\left|\beta\right|+\left|\gamma\right|=\left|\alpha\right|-1}c_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\left(x\right)\left[\int\Phi_{k}^{\lambda}\left(x-y\right)\left\{\left(\frac{y-a}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\gamma}\right\}\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\left(y\right)d\sigma\left(y\right)\right]\left(\frac{x-a}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\beta},

where
\Phi_{k}^{\lambda}\left(x-y\right)=K^{\lambda}\left(x-y\right)\left(\frac{x_{k}-y_{k}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)
. So
\left[P_{\alpha,a,I^{\prime}},T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\left(x\right)
is a ‘polynomial’ of degree
\left|\alpha\right|-1
with
variable
coefficients. Now we take the
inner product of the commutator with
\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g

for some
J\subset I^{\prime}
, and split the inner product into two pieces,

(6.7)

\displaystyle\left\langle\left[P_{\alpha,a,I^{\prime}}\ ,T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left[P_{\alpha,a,I^{\prime}}\ ,T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\left(x\right)\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left[P_{\alpha,a,I^{\prime}}\ ,T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J}\left(x\right)\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left[P_{\alpha,a,I^{\prime}}\ ,T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}\cap 2J}\left(x\right)\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\flat}\left(J\right),

where we are suppressing the dependence on
\alpha
and
I^{\prime}
. For
the first term
\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)
we write

\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)=\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{\left|\beta\right|+\left|\gamma\right|=\left|\alpha\right|-1}c_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}_{k,\beta,\gamma}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right),

where with the choice
a=c_{J}
the center of
J
, we define

(6.8)

\displaystyle\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}_{k,\beta,\gamma}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\int_{J}\left[\int_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J}\Phi_{k}^{\lambda}\left(x-y\right)\left(\frac{y-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\gamma}d\sigma\left(y\right)\right]\left(\frac{x-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\beta}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J}\left\{\int_{J}\Phi_{k}^{\lambda}\left(x-y\right)\left(\frac{x-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\beta}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)\right\}\left(\frac{y-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\gamma}d\sigma\left(y\right).

While these integrals need no longer vanish, we will show they are suitably
small, using that the function
\left(\frac{x-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\beta}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)
is supported in
J
and has vanishing
\omega
-means up to order

\kappa-\left|\beta\right|-1
, and that the function
\Phi_{k}^{\lambda}\left(z\right)
is appropriately smooth away from
z=0
,

\left|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{k}^{\lambda}\left(z\right)\right|\leq C_{m,n}\frac{1}{\left|z\right|^{m+n-\lambda-1}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}.

Indeed, we have the following estimate for the integral in braces in (
6.8
), keeping in mind that
y\in I^{\prime}\setminus 2J
and
x\in J
, where the term in the second line below vanishes because
h\left(x\right)=\left(\frac{x-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\beta}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)
has
vanishing
\omega
-means up to order
\kappa-1-\left|\beta\right|
, and the fourth line uses that
h\left(x\right)
is
supported in
J
:

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\Phi_{k}^{\lambda}\left(x-y\right)h\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\left\{\sum_{m=0}^{\kappa-\left|\beta\right|-1}\frac{1}{m!}\left(\left(x-c_{J}\right)\cdot\nabla\right)^{m}\Phi_{k}^{\lambda}\left(c_{J}-y\right)\right\}h\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)\right.

\displaystyle+\left.\int_{\mathbb{R}^{n}}\frac{1}{\left(\kappa-\left|\beta\right|\right)!}\left(\left(x-c_{J}\right)\cdot\nabla\right)^{\kappa-\left|\beta\right|}\Phi_{k}^{\lambda}\left(\eta_{J}^{\omega}\right)h\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left\|h\right\|_{L^{1}\left(\omega\right)}\frac{\ell\left(J\right)^{\kappa-\left|\beta\right|}}{\left[\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,J\right)\right]^{\kappa-\left|\beta\right|+n-\lambda-1}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\left|\beta\right|}\frac{\ell\left(J\right)^{\kappa-\left|\beta\right|}}{\left[\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,J\right)\right]^{\kappa-\left|\beta\right|+n-\lambda-1}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|,

since

\displaystyle\left\|h\right\|_{L^{1}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{J}\left|\left(\frac{x-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\beta}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)\right|d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\left|\beta\right|}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{1}\left(\omega\right)}\lesssim\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\left|\beta\right|}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|.

Now recall the orthonormal basis
\left\{h_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma,a}\right\}_{a\in\Gamma}
of
L_{I;\kappa}^{2}\left(\sigma\right)
for any
I\in\mathcal{D}
. For a
\mathcal{D}
-child
I^{\prime}
of a cube
I
, we
consider the polynomial

Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa}^{\sigma}\equiv\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\sum_{a\in\Gamma}c_{a}h_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma,a}

where
c_{a}=\frac{\left\langle f,h_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma,a}\right\rangle}{\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|}
, so that
Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa}^{\sigma}
is a renormalization of the polynomial
M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}
introduced earlier. We have

\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\sum_{a\in\Gamma}c_{a}h_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma,a}=\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa}^{\sigma}=\mathbb{E}_{I^{\prime};\kappa}^{\sigma}f-\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma}f,

where

\sum_{a\in\Gamma}\left|c_{a}\right|^{2}=\sum_{a\in\Gamma}\left|\frac{\left\langle f,h_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma,a}\right\rangle}{\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|}\right|^{2}=1.

Recall also that from (
2.9
) we have

\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|\left\|Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa}^{\sigma}\right\|_{\infty}=\left\|\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\sum_{a\in\Gamma}\left\langle f,h_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma,a}\right\rangle h_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma,a}\right\|_{\infty}=\left\|\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\bigtriangleup_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{\infty}\approx\frac{\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|}{\sqrt{\left|I\right|_{\sigma}}}.

Hence for
c_{J}\in J\subset I^{\prime}
, if we write

(6.9)

Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa}^{\sigma}\left(y\right)=\sum_{\left|\alpha\right|<\kappa}b_{\alpha}\left(\frac{y-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\alpha}=\sum_{\left|\alpha\right|<\kappa}b_{\alpha}P_{\alpha,c_{J}}\left(y\right),

and then rescale to the unit cube and invoke the fact that any two norms on
a finite dimensional vector space are equivalent, we obtain

(6.10)

\sum_{\left|\alpha\right|<\kappa}\left|b_{\alpha}\right|\approx\left\|Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa}^{\sigma}\right\|_{\infty}\approx\frac{1}{\sqrt{\left|I\right|_{\sigma}}},\ \ \ \ \ I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right).

We then write

(6.11)

\left\langle\left[Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa},T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}=\sum_{\left|\alpha\right|<\kappa}b_{\alpha}\left\langle\left[P_{\alpha,c_{J}},T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\ ,

and note that

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle\left[Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa},T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|\leq\sum_{\left|\alpha\right|<\kappa}\left|b_{\alpha}\left\langle\left[P_{\alpha,c_{J}},T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{\left|I\right|_{\sigma}}}\max_{\left|\alpha\right|<\kappa}\left|\left\langle\left[P_{\alpha,c_{J}},T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|,

so that it remains to estimate each inner product
\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)=\left\langle\left[P_{\alpha,c_{J}},T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}
as follows:

\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)\right|=\left|\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{\left|\beta\right|+\left|\gamma\right|=\left|\alpha\right|-1}c_{\alpha,\beta,\gamma}\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}_{k,\beta,\gamma}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)\right|\lesssim\max_{\left|\beta\right|+\left|\gamma\right|=\left|\alpha\right|-1}\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}_{k,\beta,\gamma}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)\right|,

where
\left|\beta\right|+\left|\gamma\right|=\left|\alpha\right|-1
, and the estimates above imply,

\displaystyle\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}_{k,\beta,\gamma}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)\right|\leq\int_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J}\left|\int\Phi_{k}^{\lambda}\left(x-y\right)\left(\frac{x-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\beta}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)\right|\left|\left(\frac{y-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\gamma}\right|d\sigma\left(y\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J}\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\left|\beta\right|}\frac{\ell\left(J\right)^{\kappa-\left|\beta\right|}}{\left[\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,J\right)\right]^{\kappa-\left|\beta\right|+n-\lambda-1}\ell\left(I\right)}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,J\right)}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\left|\gamma\right|}d\sigma\left(y\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J}\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\left|\alpha\right|-1}\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,J\right)}\right)^{\kappa-\left|\alpha\right|+1}\frac{1}{\left[\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,J\right)\right]^{n-\lambda-1}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|d\sigma\left(y\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\left|\alpha\right|-1}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|\left\{\int_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J}\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,J\right)}\right)^{\kappa-\left|\alpha\right|+1}\frac{1}{\left[\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,J\right)\right]^{n-\lambda-1}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}d\sigma\left(y\right)\right\}.

Now we fix
t\in\mathbb{N}
, and estimate the sum of
\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)\right|
over
J\subset I^{\prime}
with
\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)
by
splitting the integration in
y
according to the size of
\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,J\right)
, to obtain the following bound:

\displaystyle\sum_{J\subset I^{\prime}:\ \ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-t\left(\left|\alpha\right|-1\right)}\sum_{J\subset I^{\prime}:\ \ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|\left\{\int_{I^{\prime}\setminus 2J}\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,J\right)}\right)^{\kappa-\left|\alpha\right|+1}\frac{d\sigma\left(y\right)}{\left[\ell\left(J\right)+\mathop{\mathrm{d}ist}\left(y,J\right)\right]^{n-\lambda-1}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-t\left(\left|\alpha\right|-1\right)}\sum_{J\subset I^{\prime}:\ \ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|\left\{\sum_{s=1}^{t}\int_{2^{s+1}J\setminus 2^{s}J}\left(2^{-s}\right)^{\kappa-\left|\alpha\right|+1}\frac{d\sigma\left(y\right)}{\left(2^{s}\ell\left(J\right)\right)^{n-\lambda-1}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-t\left|\alpha\right|}\sum_{J\subset I^{\prime}:\ \ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(2^{-s}\right)^{\kappa-\left|\alpha\right|+1}2^{-s\left(n-\lambda-1\right)}\frac{\left|2^{s}J\right|_{\sigma}}{\ell\left(J\right)^{n-\lambda}},

which gives upon pigeonholing the sum in
J
according to membership in the
grandchildren of
I^{\prime}
at depth
t-s
:

\displaystyle\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-t\left|\alpha\right|}\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(2^{-s}\right)^{\kappa-\left|\alpha\right|+n-\lambda}\frac{\left|2^{s}J\right|_{\sigma}}{\ell\left(J\right)^{n-\lambda}}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle 2^{-t\left|\alpha\right|}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(2^{-s}\right)^{\kappa-\left|\alpha\right|}\sum_{K\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t-s\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(K\right)}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|\frac{\left|2^{s}J\right|_{\sigma}}{\ell\left(K\right)^{n-\lambda}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-t\left|\alpha\right|}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(2^{-s}\right)^{\kappa-\left|\alpha\right|}\sum_{K\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t-s\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\frac{\left|3K\right|_{\sigma}}{\ell\left(K\right)^{n-\lambda}}\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(K\right)}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-t\left|\alpha\right|}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(2^{-s}\right)^{\kappa-\left|\alpha\right|}\sum_{K\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t-s\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\frac{\left|3K\right|_{\sigma}}{\ell\left(K\right)^{n-\lambda}}\sqrt{\left|K\right|_{\omega}}\sqrt{\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(K\right)}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|^{2}}.

Now we use the
A_{2}^{\lambda}
condition and doubling for
\sigma
to
obtain the bound

\frac{\left|3K\right|_{\sigma}}{\ell\left(K\right)^{n-\lambda}}\sqrt{\left|K\right|_{\omega}}\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{\left|3K\right|_{\sigma}\left|3K\right|_{\omega}}}{\ell\left(3K\right)^{n-\lambda}}\sqrt{\left|3K\right|_{\sigma}}\leq\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\sqrt{\left|3K\right|_{\sigma}}\lesssim\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\sqrt{\left|K\right|_{\sigma}}.

Thus we have

\displaystyle\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-t\left|\alpha\right|}\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(2^{-s}\right)^{\kappa-\left|\alpha\right|}\sum_{K\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t-s\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\sqrt{\left|K\right|_{\sigma}}\sqrt{\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(s\right)}\left(K\right)}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-t\left|\alpha\right|}\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\sum_{s=1}^{t}\left(2^{-s}\right)^{\kappa-\left|\alpha\right|}\sqrt{\left|I^{\prime}\right|_{\sigma}}\sqrt{\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-t}\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\sqrt{\left|I^{\prime}\right|_{\sigma}}\sqrt{\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|^{2}},

since
1\leq\left|\alpha\right|\leq\kappa-1
(the commutator
vanishes if
\left|\alpha\right|=0
) shows that both
2^{-t\left|\alpha\right|}\leq 2^{-t}
and

\kappa-\left|\alpha\right|\geq 1>0.

We now claim the same estimate holds for the sum of
\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\flat}\left(J\right)\right|
over
J\subset I^{\prime}

with
\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)
, namely

\displaystyle\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}_{k,\beta,\gamma}^{\lambda,\flat}\left(J\right)\right|\lesssim\left|\int_{J}\left(\int_{2J}\Phi_{k}^{\lambda}\left(x-y\right)\left(\frac{y-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\gamma}d\sigma\left(y\right)\right)\left(\frac{x-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\beta}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)d\omega\left(x\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{J}\left(\int_{2J}\frac{1}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)\left|x-y\right|^{n-\lambda-1}}\left|\frac{y-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right|^{\left|\gamma\right|}d\sigma\left(y\right)\right)\left|\frac{x-c_{J}}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right|^{\left|\beta\right|}\left|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\left(x\right)\right|d\omega\left(x\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\left|\gamma\right|+\left|\beta\right|}\frac{1}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|\int_{J}\int_{2J}\frac{d\sigma\left(y\right)d\omega\left(x\right)}{\left|x-y\right|^{n-\lambda-1}}.

In order to estimate the double integral using the
A_{2}^{\lambda}

condition we cover the band
\left|x-y\right|\leq C2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)
by a collection of cubes
Q\left(z_{m},C2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)\times Q\left(z_{m},C2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)

in
CJ\times CJ
with centers
\left(z_{m},z_{m}\right)
and bounded
overlap. Then we have

\displaystyle\int_{J}\int_{2J}\frac{d\sigma\left(y\right)d\omega\left(x\right)}{\left|x-y\right|^{n-\lambda-1}}\leq\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\mathop{\displaystyle\iint}\limits_{\begin{subarray}{c}x,y\in 2J\\
\left|x-y\right|\approx 2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\end{subarray}}\frac{d\sigma\left(y\right)d\omega\left(x\right)}{\left(2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)^{n-\lambda-1}}

\displaystyle\approx

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\sum_{Q\left(z_{m},C2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)\times Q\left(z_{m},C2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)}\int_{Q\left(z_{m},C2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)\times Q\left(z_{m},C2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)}\frac{d\sigma\left(y\right)d\omega\left(x\right)}{\left(2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)^{n-\lambda-1}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\ell\left(J\right)^{n-\lambda-1}}\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}2^{m\left(n-\lambda-1\right)}\sum_{z_{m}}\left|Q\left(z_{m},C2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)\right|_{\sigma}\left|Q\left(z_{m},C2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)\right|_{\omega}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\ell\left(J\right)^{n-\lambda-1}}\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}2^{m\left(n-\lambda-1\right)}\sum_{z_{m}}\sqrt{\left|Q\left(z_{m},C2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)\right|_{\sigma}\left|Q\left(z_{m},C2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)\right|_{\omega}}\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\left(2^{-m}\ell\left(J\right)\right)^{n-\lambda}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\ell\left(J\right)\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}2^{m\left(n-\lambda-1\right)}2^{-m\left(n-\lambda\right)}\sqrt{\left|CJ\right|_{\sigma}\left|CJ\right|_{\omega}}\lesssim\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\ell\left(J\right)\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\sigma}\left|J\right|_{\omega}}.

Thus altogether, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}_{k,\beta,\gamma}^{\lambda,\flat}\left(J\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\left|\gamma\right|+\left|\beta\right|}\frac{1}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\ell\left(J\right)\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\sigma}\left|J\right|_{\omega}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\right)^{\left|\alpha\right|}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\sigma}}\leq\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\sigma}},

since
\left|\alpha\right|\geq 1
(otherwise the commutator
vanishes). Now

\displaystyle\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\flat}\left(J\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left[\ell\left(J\right)^{-s}\ell\left(J\right)^{s}\right]\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\sigma}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)^{-s}2^{-t\left(1-s\right)}\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\sqrt{\left|I^{\prime}\right|_{\sigma}}\sqrt{\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|^{2}},

and so altogether we have

\displaystyle\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\left\langle\left[P_{\alpha,c_{J}},T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda}\left(J\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\natural}\left(J\right)\right|+\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\mathop{\mathrm{I}nt}^{\lambda,\flat}\left(J\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-t\left(1-s\right)}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)^{-s}\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\sqrt{\left|I^{\prime}\right|_{\sigma}}\sqrt{\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|^{2}}.

Finally then we obtain from this and (
6.10
),

\displaystyle\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\left\langle\left[Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa},T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\left|\sum_{\left|\alpha\right|\leq\kappa-1}b_{\alpha}\left\langle\left[P_{\alpha,c_{J}},T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{-t\left(1-s\right)}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)^{-s}\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\sqrt{\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I^{\prime}\right)}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|^{2}}.

Now using
M_{I_{J};\kappa}=\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|Q_{I_{J};\kappa}
, and applying the above estimates with
I^{\prime}=I_{J}
, we can sum over
t
and
I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}
to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{c}ommutator};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\right|\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|\left|\left\langle\left[T_{\sigma}^{\lambda},Q_{I_{J};\kappa}\right]\mathbf{1}_{I_{{}_{J}}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{t=r}^{\infty}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}}2^{-t\left(1-s\right)}\ell\left(I^{\prime}\right)^{-s}\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|\sqrt{\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I_{J}\right)\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\sum_{t=r}^{\infty}2^{-t\left(1-s\right)}\sqrt{\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}}\ell\left(I\right)^{-2s}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|^{2}}\sqrt{\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}}\sum_{J\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}^{\left(t\right)}\left(I_{J}\right)\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\ell\left(J\right)^{2s}\left|\widehat{g}\left(J\right)\right|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\sum_{t=r}^{\infty}2^{-t}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{s}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}\lesssim\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{s}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\mathbf{\tau}-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}.

Thus the commutator form
\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{c}ommutator};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\,
is controlled by
A_{2}^{\lambda}
alone.

Finally using orthogonality of both
\left\{\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}}^{\sigma}f\right\}_{F\in\mathcal{F}}
and
\left\{\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}\right\}_{F\in\mathcal{F}}
, we obtain

(6.12)

\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{c}ommutator};\kappa}\left(f,g\right)\right|\lesssim\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\left\|f\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{s}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)},\ \ \ \ \ \left|s\right|<1.

6.3.
The neighbour form

In this form we can obtain the required bound, which uses only the
A_{2}^{\lambda}
constant, by taking absolute values inside the sum, and
following
[
AlSaUr
]
again, we argue as in the case of Haar wavelets in

[
SaShUr7
, end of Subsection 8.4]
. We begin with
M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}=\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\bigtriangleup_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma}f
as in (
6.1
) to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{n}eighbour};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\sum_{\theta\left(I_{J}\right)\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)\setminus\left\{I_{J}\right\}}\left|\left\langle T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\left(\mathbf{1}_{\theta\left(I_{J}\right)}\bigtriangleup_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma}f\right),\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\sum_{I^{\prime}\equiv\theta\left(I_{J}\right)\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)\setminus\left\{I_{J}\right\}}\left|\left\langle T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\left(M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\right),\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|.

We now control this by the pivotal bound (
3.14
) on the inner
product with
\nu=\left\|M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\sigma\right)}\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}d\sigma
, and then
estimating by the usual Poisson kernel,

\displaystyle\left|\left\langle T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\left(M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\right),\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathrm{P}_{\kappa}^{\lambda}\left(J,\left\|M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\sigma\right)}\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\sigma\right)\ell\left(J\right)^{-s}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left\|M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\sigma\right)}\mathrm{P}^{\lambda}\left(J,\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\sigma\right)\ell\left(J\right)^{-s}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)},

and the estimate
\left\|M_{I^{\prime};\kappa}\right\|_{L^{\infty}\left(\sigma\right)}\approx\frac{1}{\sqrt{\left|I^{\prime}\right|_{\sigma}}}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|

from (
2.9
), along with (
3.15
), namely

\mathrm{P}_{m}^{\lambda}(J,\sigma\mathbf{1}_{K\setminus I})\lesssim\left(\frac{\ell\left(J\right)}{\ell\left(I\right)}\right)^{m-\varepsilon\left(n+m-\lambda\right)}\mathrm{P}_{m}^{\alpha}(I,\sigma\mathbf{1}_{K\setminus I}),

to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{n}eighbour};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\right|\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}\text{ and }J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\sum_{I^{\prime}\equiv\theta\left(I_{J}\right)\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)\setminus\left\{I_{J}\right\}}\frac{\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|}{\sqrt{\left|I^{\prime}\right|_{\sigma}}}\mathrm{P}^{\lambda}\left(J,\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\sigma\right)\ell\left(J\right)^{-s}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{0},I_{\theta}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)\\
I_{0}\neq I_{\theta}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\text{ and }J\subset I_{0}\end{subarray}}\frac{\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|}{\sqrt{\left|I_{\theta}\right|_{\sigma}}}\mathrm{P}^{\lambda}\left(J,\mathbf{1}_{I_{\theta}}\sigma\right)\ell\left(J\right)^{-s}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=r}^{\infty}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{0},I_{\theta}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)\\
I_{0}\neq I_{\theta}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\text{ and }\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I\right)\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\text{ and }J\subset I_{0}\end{subarray}}\frac{\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|}{\sqrt{\left|I_{\theta}\right|_{\sigma}}}\mathrm{P}^{\lambda}\left(J,\mathbf{1}_{I_{\theta}}\sigma\right)\ell\left(J\right)^{-s}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{t=r}^{\infty}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{0},I_{\theta}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)\\
I_{0}\neq I_{\theta}\end{subarray}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\text{ and }\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I\right)\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\text{ and }J\subset I_{0}\end{subarray}}\frac{\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|}{\sqrt{\left|I_{\theta}\right|_{\sigma}}}\left\{\left(2^{-t}\right)^{1-\varepsilon\left(n+1-\lambda\right)-s}\ell\left(I\right)^{s}\mathrm{P}^{\lambda}\left(I_{0},\mathbf{1}_{I_{\theta}}\sigma\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{0},I_{\theta}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)\\
I_{0}\neq I_{\theta}\end{subarray}}\sum_{t=r}^{\infty}A\left(I,I_{0},I_{\theta},t\right),

where

A\left(I,I_{0},I_{\theta},t\right)=\left(2^{-t}\right)^{1-\varepsilon\left(n+1-\lambda\right)-s}\frac{\ell\left(I\right)^{s}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|}{\sqrt{\left|I^{\prime}\right|_{\sigma}}}\mathrm{P}^{\lambda}\left(I_{0},\mathbf{1}_{I_{\theta}}\sigma\right)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\mathbf{\tau}-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\text{ and }\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I\right)\\
J\Subset_{\mathbf{\rho},\varepsilon}I\text{ and }J\subset I_{0}\end{subarray}}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\ell\left(J\right)^{s}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}.

Now recall that the case
s=0
of the following estimate was proved in
[
SaShUr7
, see from the bottom of page 120 to the top of page 122]
,

\left|\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}I_{0},I_{\theta}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)\\
I_{0}\neq I_{\theta}\end{subarray}}\sum_{s=r}^{\infty}A\left(I,I_{0},I_{\theta},s\right)\right|\lesssim\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{s}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)},

where the quantity
A\left(I,I_{0},I_{\theta},t\right)
was defined there
with
s=0
(in our notation) by

\left(2^{-t}\right)^{1-\varepsilon\left(n+1-\lambda\right)}\left|E_{I_{\theta}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I;1}^{\sigma}f\right|\mathrm{P}^{\lambda}\left(I_{0},\mathbf{1}_{I_{\theta}}\sigma\right)\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\text{ and }\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I\right)\\
J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\text{ and }J\subset I_{0}\end{subarray}}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{L^{2}\left(\omega\right)}.

When
\sigma
is doubling, the reader can check that
\left|E_{I_{\theta}}^{\sigma}\bigtriangleup_{I;1}^{\sigma}f\right|\approx\frac{\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|}{\sqrt{\left|I_{\theta}\right|_{\sigma}}}
, and then that the proof in
[
SaShUr7
, see
from the bottom of page 120 to the top of page 122]
applies almost
verbatim to our situation when
\left|\varepsilon\left(n+1-\lambda\right)+s\right|<1
. This proves the required bound for the neighbour
form,

\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{n}eighbour};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\right|\lesssim\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{s}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)},\ \ \ \ \ \left|s\right|<1,

since we can always take
\varepsilon\left(n+1-\lambda\right)
as small
as we wish.

Finally using orthogonality of both
\left\{\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}}^{\sigma}f\right\}_{F\in\mathcal{F}}
and
\left\{\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}\right\}_{F\in\mathcal{F}}
, we obtain

(6.13)

\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{n}eighbour};\kappa}\left(f,g\right)\right|\lesssim\sqrt{A_{2}^{\lambda}}\left\|f\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{s}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)},\ \ \ \ \ \left|s\right|<1.

6.4.
The stopping form

To bound the stopping form, we follow the argument for the Haar stopping
form due to Nazarov, Treil and Volberg as in
[
AlSaUr
]
. However, we only
need the classical
1
-pivotal constant
\mathcal{V}_{2}^{\lambda,1}

(defined in (
3.9
) for
\kappa=1
) for this, not the
\left(\beta,\varepsilon^{\prime}\right)
-strong
1
-pivotal constant
\mathcal{V}_{2,\beta,\varepsilon^{\prime}}^{\lambda,1}
(defined in (
3.10
) for
\kappa=1
and
\varepsilon=\varepsilon^{\prime}
), since there is no use of quasiorthogonality in this argument, only
orthogonality. In view of this consideration, we apply the
1
-pivotal
stopping time construction to the Alpert projection
\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F};\kappa}^{\sigma}f
in order to obtain a further corona decomposition
\mathcal{C}_{F}=\bigcup_{H\in\mathcal{H}\left(F\right)}\mathcal{C}_{H}

in which we obtain the stopping control bound

(6.14)

\mathrm{P}_{1}^{\lambda}\left(I,\mathbf{1}_{H}\sigma\right)^{2}\left|I\right|_{\omega}\leq\Gamma\left|I\right|_{\sigma}\lesssim 2\mathcal{V}_{2}^{\lambda,1}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)\left|I\right|_{\sigma},\ \ \ \ \ I\in\mathcal{C}_{H}\ ,\ H\in\mathcal{H}\left(F\right),\ F\in\mathcal{F}.

Recall that

\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa}=\mathbb{E}_{I^{\prime};\kappa}^{\sigma}f-\mathbf{1}_{I^{\prime}}\mathbb{E}_{I;\kappa}^{\sigma}f.

We begin the proof by pigeonholing the ratio of side lengths of
I
and
J

in the stopping form:

\displaystyle\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\equiv\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{F}}\sum_{I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\\
J\subset I^{\prime}\text{ and }J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|\left\langle Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa}T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{F\setminus I^{\prime}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{H\in\mathcal{H}\left(F\right)}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{H}}\sum_{I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\cap\mathcal{C}_{H}\\
J\subset I^{\prime}\text{ and }J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|\left\langle Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa}T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{H\setminus I^{\prime}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\sum_{H\in\mathcal{H}\left(F\right)}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{H}}\sum_{I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\cap\mathcal{C}_{H}\ \text{and }\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I\right)\\
J\subset I^{\prime}\text{ and }J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|\left\langle Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa}T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{H\setminus I^{\prime}},\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}

\displaystyle\equiv

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{\infty}\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top};\kappa,t}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\ ,

where using Lemma
27
, we have
\left\|\left|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right|\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}\lesssim\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}
. Thus using the fact that
Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}
has one vanishing moment of order
0
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top};\kappa,t}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\right|\leq\sum_{H\in\mathcal{H}\left(F\right)}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{H}}\sum_{I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\cap\mathcal{C}_{H}\text{ and }\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I\right)\\
J\subset I^{\prime}\text{ and }J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|\ \left|\left\langle T_{\sigma}^{\lambda}\mathbf{1}_{H\setminus I^{\prime}},Q_{I^{\prime};\kappa}\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\rangle_{\omega}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{H\in\mathcal{H}\left(F\right)}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{H}}\sum_{I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\cap\mathcal{C}_{H}\text{ and }\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I\right)\\
J\subset I^{\prime}\text{ and }J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\left|I^{\prime}\right|_{\sigma}}}\ \mathrm{P}_{1}^{\lambda}\left(J,\mathbf{1}_{H\setminus I^{\prime}}\sigma\right)\ell\left(J\right)^{-s}\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{H\in\mathcal{H}\left(F\right)}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{H}}\sum_{I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\cap\mathcal{C}_{H}\text{ and }\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I\right)\\
J\subset I^{\prime}\text{ and }J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\ell\left(I\right)^{-s}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\left|I^{\prime}\right|_{\sigma}}}\ \left(2^{-t}\right)^{1-\varepsilon\left(n+1-\lambda\right)-s}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times\mathrm{P}_{1}^{\lambda}\left(I,\mathbf{1}_{H\setminus I^{\prime}}\sigma\right)\sqrt{\left|J\right|_{\omega}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J;\kappa}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(2^{-t}\right)^{1-\varepsilon\left(n+1-\lambda\right)-s}\sqrt{\sum_{H\in\mathcal{H}\left(F\right)}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{H}}\sum_{I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\cap\mathcal{C}_{H}\text{ and }\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I\right)\\
J\subset I^{\prime}\text{ and }J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\ell\left(I\right)^{-2s}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|^{2}\frac{1}{\left|I^{\prime}\right|_{\sigma}}\ \mathrm{P}_{1}^{\lambda}\left(I,\mathbf{1}_{H\setminus I^{\prime}}\sigma\right)^{2}\left|J\right|_{\omega}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times\sqrt{\sum_{H\in\mathcal{H}\left(F\right)}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{H}}\sum_{I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\cap\mathcal{C}_{H}\text{ and }\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I\right)\\
J\subset I^{\prime}\text{ and }J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}}\ .

Now we note that

\sum_{H\in\mathcal{H}\left(F\right)}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{H}}\sum_{I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\text{and }\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-s}\ell\left(I\right)\\
J\subset I^{\prime}\text{
and }J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}\leq\sum_{J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}\left\|\bigtriangleup_{J}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}=\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}^{2}\ ,

and use the stopping control bound
\mathrm{P}_{1}^{\lambda}\left(I,\mathbf{1}_{H}\sigma\right)^{2}\left|I\right|_{\omega}\leq\Gamma\left|I\right|_{\sigma}\lesssim 2\mathcal{V}_{2}^{\lambda,1}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)\left|I^{\prime}\right|_{\sigma}
in the corona
\mathcal{C}_{H}
, to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top};\kappa,t}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(2^{-t\left(1-s\right)}\right)^{1-\varepsilon\left(n+1-\lambda\right)}\mathcal{V}_{2}^{\lambda,1}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)

\displaystyle\times\sqrt{\sum_{H\in\mathcal{H}\left(F\right)}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{H}}\sum_{I^{\prime}\in\mathfrak{C}_{\mathcal{D}}\left(I\right)}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}J\in\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}\text{and }\ell\left(J\right)=2^{-t}\ell\left(I\right)\\
J\subset I^{\prime}\text{
and }J\Subset_{\rho,\varepsilon}I\end{subarray}}\ell\left(I\right)^{-2s}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|^{2}\frac{\left|J\right|_{\sigma}}{\left|I^{\prime}\right|_{\sigma}}}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(2^{-t\left(1-s\right)}\right)^{1-\varepsilon\left(n+1-\lambda\right)}\mathcal{V}_{2}^{\lambda,1}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)\sqrt{\sum_{H\in\mathcal{H}\left(F\right)}\sum_{I\in\mathcal{C}_{H}}\ell\left(I\right)^{-2s}\left|\widehat{f}\left(I\right)\right|^{2}}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(2^{-t}\right)^{1-\varepsilon\left(n+1-\lambda\right)-s}\mathcal{V}_{2}^{\lambda,1}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{s}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}.

Finally then we sum in
t
to obtain

\displaystyle\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top};\kappa}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\right|\leq\mathcal{V}_{2,\varepsilon}^{\lambda,1}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left|\mathsf{B}_{\mathop{\mathrm{s}top};\kappa,t}^{F}\left(f,g\right)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathcal{V}_{2,\varepsilon}^{\lambda,1}\left(\sigma,\omega\right)\sum_{t=0}^{\infty}\left(2^{-t}\right)^{1-\varepsilon\left(n+1-\lambda\right)-s}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}}^{\sigma}f\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{s}\left(\sigma\right)}\left\|\mathsf{P}_{\mathcal{C}_{F}^{\tau-\mathop{\mathrm{s}hift}}}^{\omega}g\right\|_{W_{\mathop{\mathrm{d}yad}}^{-s}\left(\omega\right)}

\displaystyle\lesssim
